### 2026-09-20 — chore/rok-1475-feature-gated-versions (surfaced during ROK-1475 spec + review)

- **[med]** `.github/workflows/release.yml:97` — two workflows create the GitHub release for the same
  tag: `release.yml`'s final step runs `gh release create "vX.Y.Z" --generate-notes`, and
  `.github/workflows/docker-publish.yml:101` runs `softprops/action-gh-release` on the `v*` tag push
  that `release.yml` just made. Both workflows are additionally *named* `Release`, so the Actions tab
  shows two same-named runs per tag and it is not obvious which produced the release. Which one wins
  is unverified — no tag has been cut since `v1.1.0`, so the race has never actually been observed.
  Expect one of them to error or silently no-op on an existing release, and the notes to come from
  whichever landed first. Suggested: pick ONE creator (keep `gh release create --generate-notes` in
  `release.yml`, drop the `softprops` step from `docker-publish.yml`, which then only publishes
  images) and rename `docker-publish.yml` to something like `Publish images` so the two runs are
  distinguishable.
- **[med]** `scripts/*.spec.mjs` — the seven node:test spec files under `scripts/` run in NO GitHub
  Actions job. They execute only via `scripts/validate-ci.sh --full` (the "Script node:test specs"
  step), and the default pre-push gate is `--static`, so in practice they run on a laptop/fleet
  `--full` escalation and nowhere else. A change that breaks one of them merges green. (Their sibling
  shell tests, `scripts/test/*.test.sh` via `scripts/test/run-all.sh`, ARE wired into the `lint` job —
  so the gap is specifically the `.mjs` specs.) Suggested: add a `node --test scripts/*.spec.mjs` step
  to the existing `lint`/scripts job in `ci.yml`, next to the `run-all.sh` invocation.

### 2026-09-20 — fix/rok-1617-anti-vote-followup (surfaced during the ROK-1475 fleet gate and the ROK-1617 follow-up Codex review)

- **[med]** `api/src/lineups/scheduling/scheduling-poll-expiry.service.ts:164` + `api/src/notifications/notification.service.ts:64-92` — a
  notification's dedup key is marked BEFORE the Discord DM is known to have gone out, and nothing ever reports a skip. `warnOne` marks
  `sched-poll-expiry-warn:{matchId}` and then calls `sendWarning`; `notificationService.create` returns `null` when the in-app channel is
  disabled and fires `dispatchDiscord` un-awaited, so a DM dropped by the Discord layer's 5-minute rate-limit bucket
  (`discord-notification.service.ts:186-197`, keyed `discord-notif:rate:{userId}:{type}:{subType}`) leaves the key marked and the DM is
  never retried. Pre-existing pattern, not this branch: the branch only stopped two DIFFERENT expiry DMs sharing one bucket (Codex P2,
  fixed by giving the no-leader DM its own `reminderWindow`). Any caller that dedups-then-dispatches has the same hole. Suggested: have
  `create` return a dispatch result (sent / skipped-rate-limited / disabled) and mark the dedup key only on `sent`, or mark with a short
  TTL and extend on success.
- **[low]** `scripts/test/run-all.sh` is not hermetic inside a fleet runner: 4 of 19 suites go red there and are green on a laptop and in
  GitHub's `lint` job. Observed twice on 2026-09-20 on slot 3 (identical before and after the branch's own changes, which touch none of
  these files): `reconcile-migrations-trust-probe.test.sh` 2 pass / 9 fail, all `✗ Halted: connect ECONNREFUSED 127.0.0.1:5448x` (its
  throwaway Postgres is not reachable from inside the runner container); `validate-ci-integration-shards.test.sh` 1 fail —
  `AC-M10-5: local mode must not spawn the M9 Redis sidecar, got 1`; `validate-ci-only-flags.test.sh` 2 fail — `AC2: --no-coverage must
  run jest with a 3072 MB heap ceiling (pattern not found)` and `AC2: --only-unit alone must keep the api coverage script (pattern not
  found: test:cov)`; `validate-ci-redis-sidecar.test.sh` 2 fail — `AC-M9-9: RL_TARGET=local must NOT spawn a sidecar` and
  `RL_TARGET=local must NOT export sidecar REDIS_URL (got REDIS_URL_AFTER=redis://rl-test-redis-3-xxx:6379)`. The runner's ambient
  `RL_TARGET` / sidecar env leaks into tests that assume a clean local shell. Root cause not proven. Suggested: have each of those
  suites scrub `RL_*` / `REDIS_URL` from its environment before exercising "local mode", and skip (loudly) the Postgres-container suite
  when no Docker socket is reachable.

### 2026-09-22 — fix/rok-1640-1641-sheet-visibility (surfaced during fleet gate `0fd77f9cf4f8`, env `rok1640a`)

- **[med — pre-existing, NOT this branch]** `scripts/smoke/profile-gaming.smoke.spec.ts:96` and `:129` (ROK-1636 control, desktop + mobile) fail on any fleet env with IGDB credentials: `getByRole('option').filter({ has: getByText('World of Warcraft Classic Era', { exact: true }) })` → `element(s) not found`. Cause: IGDB enrichment renames the seeded row. On `rok1640a`, `games.id=2` (`slug world-of-warcraft-classic`, `igdb_id 75379`) reads `name='World of Warcraft Classic'` with `short_name='WoW Classic Era'` and `igdb_enrichment_status='enriched'` (`cached_at` 22:12:03, two minutes before the gate). Migration `0091_add_api_namespace_prefix.sql:21-25` set the curated name, and `seed-games.helpers.ts::buildSeedGameUpdateSet` never rewrites `name`, so the IGDB sync overwrites it. GitHub CI has no IGDB keys, so main stays green. The branch diff touches no `api/**` or seed code. Product side effect: prod users may see "World of Warcraft Classic" rather than "Classic Era" once enrichment runs. Suggested: have IGDB enrichment keep `name` on registry/seeded games (slug in `GAMES_SEED`), or add `name` to the seed re-apply set. In the meantime, the spec could match on `WoW Classic Era` or the slug.

User 112 is the smoke operator identity from ROK-1628, linked to the FAKE Discord id `100000000000001628`; the grace test's `pickThirdParticipant` can pick it. Any DM to that user fails with Discord 10013 "Unknown User", and `api/src/notifications/discord-notification.processor.ts:44` responds by permanently deactivating the user, after which invitee validation (`api/src/lineups/lineups-invitees.helpers.ts:76`, `activeUsersFilter()`) rejects it. ROK-1619's diff touches no lineup, user or smoke-setup code; the same test passed on main runs 35858357399 and 35840684489. Suggested: exclude the fake-id operator identity from `pickThirdParticipant` (or give smoke seed users a flag the 10013 deactivation path skips), and assert the picked user is active before `POST /lineups`.

### 2026-09-23 — feat/rok-1435-digest-l1 (surfaced during its fleet full gate)

- **med — pre-existing, NOT ROK-1435; now also fails on `[desktop]`** — `scripts/smoke/scheduling-poll-live-updates.smoke.spec.ts:193` `[desktop]` + `[mobile]`, "a vote cast in context A appears in context B without reload": `expect(locator).toHaveText("1 vote")` received `"0 votes"` after `LIVE_BOUND_MS` = 10s (`:214`). It failed on both attempts in fleet gate `c95f3834011e` (slot 1, env `rok1435a` @ `746c56d67`). The 2026-09-23 ROK-1646 entry above covers `[mobile]` only and records desktop as green, so this is the first desktop failure. **Mechanism, from Loki on `rl-env-rok1435a-allinone` during the Playwright run (17:27–17:47Z):**
  - B hears about a vote only after the queue: vote → `SchedulingPollEmbedQueueService.enqueue` (queue `scheduling-poll-embed-sync`, 2s coalesce) → `SchedulingPollEmbedProcessor` (`api/src/lineups/scheduling/scheduling-poll-embed.processor.ts:25`, no `concurrency`, so the BullMQ default of 1) → `syncEmbed` → `emitScheduleChanged` (`scheduling-poll-embed.service.ts:240`) → Discord `editEmbed` (`api/src/discord-bot/discord-bot-client.service.ts:204`, which has no timeout of its own).
  - `SchedulingPollExpiryService` (`@Cron` every 5 minutes) logged "Poll expiry sweep: 0 warning(s), 20 re-render(s)" at 17:35:00 and "21 re-render(s)" at 17:41:52. Each sweep puts 20+ Discord card edits on that concurrency-1 queue.
  - At 17:35:38: `ERROR [DemoTestCore] await-processing failed after 30000ms — busy=[{"name":"scheduling-poll-embed-sync","waiting":0,"active":1,...}]`. The env's bot uses the shared CI guild and logged repeated "Discord API timeout" errors in the same window.
  - A vote cast during a sweep waits behind those edits before its `lineup:schedule-changed` nudge is sent. B's socket stays connected, so `web/src/hooks/use-schedule-poll-realtime.ts`'s 10s fallback refetch (which runs only while the socket is disconnected) never fires. The gateway and processor logged no errors: the event arrives late, it is not lost.
  - The sweep re-renders every expired poll, so the backlog grows with DB age. That fits the ROK-1646 entry's "worse on a DB that has been through a full run".
  - **Why not this branch:** `git diff origin/main...HEAD` changes nothing under `api/src/lineups/**`, `api/src/queue`, `app.module.ts`, `main.ts`, `use-schedule-poll-realtime.ts`, `use-lineup-realtime.ts` or `web/src/components/lineups`. The module edits only add providers and controllers: `WeeklyDigestSettingsController`, `WeeklyDigestService` and `DemoTestWeeklyDigestController`. `WeeklyDigestService` uses db, redis, settings and cron only, with no gateway, queue or emitter. Main `abde164b4` already failed `:193 [mobile]` on a fresh env in 2 of 3 first attempts (task `8eb41334bf2d`). GitHub main runs 35891269073 and 35885147360 passed this spec on fresh DBs.
  - Suggested (own story; do not raise the timeout): send `lineup:schedule-changed` directly from the vote path instead of only from the queued `syncEmbed`, OR move the sweep's bulk re-renders to a lower-priority job/queue (or raise the processor's concurrency), AND bound `editEmbed` with a timeout. Reproduce first per the flake protocol: loop the spec on a post-full-run DB across a :x0/:x5 sweep.
- **med — pre-existing, NOT ROK-1435; same root cause as the bullet above** — `scripts/smoke/scheduling-poll-live-updates.smoke.spec.ts:222` `[mobile]`, "a vote retracted via the API disappears from context B without reload": `expect(locator).toHaveText("0 votes")` received `"1 vote"` after 10s (`:235`). It failed on both attempts in gate `c95f3834011e`. The ROK-1646 entry records `:222` as green, so this is the first failure there. The retract nudge takes the same concurrency-1 `scheduling-poll-embed-sync` path and was delayed behind the same 20- and 21-card expiry sweeps. Suggested: the same fix as above. The spec needs no change of its own.
- **low — pre-existing, surfaced in ROK-1435 review** — `api/src/notifications/notification-dedup.service.ts:64` `releaseKey` deletes the Redis key before the DB row. If a `checkAndMarkSent` for the same key runs between the two deletes, it misses Redis, finds the DB row still there, and re-warms Redis (`warmRedisCache`). The DB delete then removes the row, leaving a Redis key with no DB claim behind it. Every later check hits Redis and reports "already sent" for the whole TTL, so the retry that the release was meant to allow never happens. For the weekly digest (TTL about 8 days) that week's post is lost. Suggested: delete the DB row first, then the Redis key. An interleaved check then sees at most one stale Redis "sent", and the next check retries.
- **med — pre-existing, NOT ROK-1435; fleet-env-only (Steam configured)** — `scripts/smoke/onboarding.smoke.spec.ts:289` `[tablet]`, "Onboarding wizard games step > genre filter chips are visible": `expect(locator).toBeVisible() failed … getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Onboarding wizard' }).getByRole('button', { name: 'All', exact: true }) … Timeout: 10000ms … Error: element(s) not found`, identical on retry #1 (gate `37c10d454079`, slot 1, env `rok1435a`). The `waitForGamesStepData` barrier had already seen the `All` chip, so the chips unmounted afterwards: both attempts' `error-context.md` show the dialog snapped back to "Step 1 of 5" with the heading "Connect Your Steam Account". Mechanism: `web/src/pages/onboarding-wizard-page.tsx:50-54` returns `needsSteamConnect = false` while `steamStatus.isLoading`, so `buildSteps` (`:80`) starts without Steam and index 0 is Games. When the steam-link query settles late, Steam is inserted at index 0 and the wizard's step index now points at it. That is the "Steam flap" the barrier's comment describes, and here it arrives after the barrier has finished. It needs `steamConfigured`, which the fleet env has and GitHub CI (no Steam key) does not, so main CI never exercises it: main runs 35891269073 and 35885147360 run all three projects (`--shard`, no `--project`) and list no onboarding failure. Why not this branch: `git diff e0e533c65 b92887cfd` touches no onboarding page, component, hook or spec, and nothing under the system-status or steam-link APIs. Suggested: key the wizard's current step by step `key`, not by index, so a late conditional step never displaces the step the user is on, or hold the step list until `systemStatus` and `steamStatus` have both settled.

### 2026-09-23 — feat/rok-1658-composer-always-list (surfaced during its fleet full gate)

- **[med — flake, NOT this branch]** `scripts/smoke/admin-user-moderation.smoke.spec.ts:62` — `expect(page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Kick user' })).toBeVisible()` "element(s) not found" on [mobile] (both attempts, fleet gate task `b00a294eacae`, env `rok1658a`) and flaky on [tablet] (passed on retry). The error-context.md snapshot for each mobile attempt shows the kebab already open (`[expanded]`) but the row reading "Deactivated", so the menu offered Reactivate/Ban instead of Kick — not a debounce/remount race or stale-token issue. Cause: the freshly-seeded `non-guild-<snowflake>` member (`seed-non-guild-user`, `api/src/admin/demo-test-deactivation.controller.ts:66`) carries a syntactically-valid Discord snowflake that is not in the test guild; on a fleet env the bot is live and shares CI's guild, so any concurrent spec's DM to that user fails and the 50278 classifier (`api/src/notifications/discord-notification-deactivate.helpers.ts`) deactivates it mid-run. GitHub CI has no live bot so this never fires there — main CI run 35891269073 (`15f3c7d8f`) was green on this spec — and ROK-1658's diff touches no deactivation/notifications/users/admin-list code (only `lfg-board-section` + `use-lfg-board-settings`). This corrects the root cause guessed at `TECH-DEBT-BACKLOG.md:344` (token revocation).
  Suggested: have `seed-non-guild-user` disable Discord DMs for the seeded member's notification preferences, and add a "not Deactivated" precondition assertion before the kebab click in the smoke spec so a future failure states the cause instead of timing out on a missing menu item.

So every public lineup creation sends deactivated, kicked and banned users a "Link your Steam account" DM, and this is the fan-out that deactivates the moderation smoke's fixture above. Its sibling `api/src/lineups/lineup-notification-targets.helpers.ts:23` already filters `u.deactivated_at IS NULL`. Suggested: use trace 'retain-on-failure' for fleet gate runs.

### 2026-09-25 — tech-debt/rok-1527-integration-heap-retainer (ROK-1527 outcome; corrects the 2026-09-11 entries)
- **[resolved — correction]** The 2026-09-11 entry "RESOLVED — the integration heap-OOM was a Node 20 problem, and Node 22 fixed it" was confounded. Its "same 4096MB cap" claim is wrong: #1161 had already raised the cap to 8192 when #1160's Node 22 run was measured. The leak survived Node 22, at about 46.5 MB per spec file (CI run 36186028645: all 51 earlier realms alive at file 52, heap 2701 MB). The real owners were two timers left running after teardown: a module-scope `setInterval` in `api/src/discord-bot/listeners/event-link.dedup.ts`, and an uncleared startup `setTimeout` in `api/src/version/version-check.service.ts`. On the fleet path (no `CI`) a third retainer was the per-spec-file Testcontainers Postgres (a followed log stream plus the Ryuk socket, created inside each file's sandbox). ROK-1527 fixes all three: lazy dedup expiry, the version-check timer cleared on destroy, and one Postgres per shard in jest `globalSetup`. It also puts `test:integration` back to `--max-old-space-size=4096`. Measured flat after the fix: CI run 36188709428 at a 4096 cap (4 shards green, peak 449 MB, ≤2.1 MB per file); fleet task 92bf79b1113f (4 shards green, peak 475 MB, ≤1.3 MB per file). The 2026-09-10/11 "deterministically red on shard 1/4" wording was also overstated: the heap sat on the edge and tipped with `randomize: true` ordering. If the climb comes back, re-run the opt-in probe (`api/src/common/testing/heap-probe.ts`, with `RL_HEAP_PROBE=1`) before guessing.

### 2026-09-26 — fix/tech-debt-batch-2026-09-26 (surfaced by the ROK-1339 re-triage)
- **[med — open operator question, NOT fixed]** `api/scripts/apply-settings-overlay.ts:129-145` (`applyOverlay`, `onConflictDoUpdate` at `:139`) — the VM bundle overlay UPSERTs every bundle key unconditionally, and `rl_env_deploy` runs it AFTER a successful laptop `sync_settings` (`tools/mcp-rl-fleet/src/tools/env-deploy-steps.ts`, step 6). So a stale `/srv/rl-infra/settings/bundle.enc` overwrites the fresher per-operator values the sync just copied for every shared (non-identity) key — ITAD/Co-Optimus/Blizzard/IGDB/LLM. Only the slot identity is meant to win over the sync. Precedence was deliberately left unchanged by this batch (the ROK-1339 fix only corrected the shared-key count and surfaced `bundle_warning` on green deploys). Suggested: operator rules whether a successful sync should win for shared keys (e.g. pass the synced key names to the overlay and skip them, or apply the bundle only when the sync failed).
- **[low — idea]** No way to see how old `bundle.enc` is without SSH. Suggested: a bundle-freshness readout in `rl_status` (bundle mtime + key-name count + whether it decrypts), so a stale or undecryptable bundle shows before a laptop-less deploy depends on it.

### 2026-09-26 — fix/tech-debt-batch-2026-09-26b (surfaced by the overnight tech-debt scout, item 30)

- med — `tools/test-bot/src/helpers/polling.ts:51` (`pollForEmbed`) / `:178` (`pollFallback`): root cause of the ~1-in-25 discord-smoke `Unterminated string in JSON at position 139778` in "Tentative signup reflected in embed" (`channel-embeds.test.ts:157`). It was discord.js parsing a ~140 KB `channel.messages.fetch({limit:100})` body inside `readLastMessages` (`helpers/messages.ts:98`). It was NOT the companion `ApiClient`, which only POSTs/DELETEs on that path. **Fixed on this branch:** a truncated Discord read is now a poll miss, not a crash, and `ApiClient` names the method, path and byte length on a truncated body. Suggested follow-up if it recurs: read fewer messages per poll (`limit` 25–50).
- ~~low — `.claude/skills/build-batch/steps/step-5-ship.md:30`, `.claude/skills/fix-batch/**` and ~8 other skill briefs still cite `tsc --noEmit -p web/tsconfig.json`. That is a solution-style config, so it checks 0 files; `validate-ci.sh` was fixed on this branch to use `web/tsconfig.app.json` + `web/tsconfig.node.json`. Suggested: operator sweeps the skill briefs (operator config).~~ **FIXED 2026-10-02** — every skill brief that runs a web typecheck now names the app/node/test leaf configs from the repo root. #1455 switched the fix-batch step-2 viability check to the app leaf; the 2026-10-02 brief sweep replaced the `-p web/tsconfig.json` cites, replaced the bare `cd web && npx tsc --noEmit` row in `/build` step 2, added the node + test leaves to fix-batch step 2, and gave the generic `-p <workspace>` placeholders a web leaf-config note. Evidence: `git grep -n 'web/tsconfig\.json' -- .claude` returns nothing, `git grep -nE 'cd (web|api)[^&;]*&& *npx tsc' -- .claude` returns nothing, and `git grep -n 'tsc --noEmit' -- .claude | grep -v -- '-p '` returns only prose mentions in `fleet-dev-lane` and `fleet-orchestrator`, no invocation.
- ~~low — `web/tsconfig.test.json` (new, ungated): `npx tsc --noEmit -p web/tsconfig.test.json` reports 291 errors in 102 spec files at 2026-09-26. `components/lineups` has the most (104); `vitest-axe` matchers need an ambient `declare module 'vitest'` augmentation. Suggested: clear it directory by directory, then gate it in `run_typecheck` and the CI lint job.~~ **FIXED 2026-10-02** — gated at 0 errors since #1427 (`run_typecheck` validate-ci.sh:663 + the CI lint job's "Typecheck (web specs)"); #1438 and #1440 kept it clean.

### 2026-09-26 — fix/tech-debt-lfm-headcount-smoke (resolutions + one follow-up from its review)

### 2026-09-27 — feat/rok-1366-magic-link-hardening (review finding, out of scope)
- **[med]** `api/src/discord-bot/listeners/signup-status.handlers.ts:245` builds `/join?intent=signup&eventId=..&token=<intent token>`, and nothing strips that query-string token from the address bar. `web/src/lib/magic-link-capture.ts` strips only the `#token=` fragment. So the intent token is in the URL when Sentry Replay starts recording, and it lands unscrubbed in the rrweb Meta `href` of error-sampled replays (`replaysOnErrorSampleRate: 1.0` in prod, `web/src/sentry.ts:33`). `beforeAddRecordingEvent` never sees Meta (type-4) events; the `beforeSend` backstop covers `request.url` only. Out of scope for ROK-1366, which hardens the magic-link fragment. Suggested: strip the intent `?token=` before `Sentry.init` the same way `magic-link-capture` does, and hand it to `web/src/pages/join-page.tsx` via memory.

### 2026-09-27 — chore/rok-1154-bundle-budget (surfaced during fleet gate)
- **[med]** `scripts/playwright-global-setup.ts:82` (fleet runner image): `[global-setup] Playwright browsers are missing or are the wrong build for this version of @playwright/test (expected at: /ms-playwright/chromium_headless_shell-1243/...)` (slot-3 fleet task 1ea74d6ff72c).
  - **Cause:** the repo pins `@playwright/test ^1.63.0`, but the rl-infra runner image bakes the browsers for an older minor. A runner's stale persistent `node_modules` masks the drift until something runs `npm ci` on it. A devDependency branch has to do that, and then the runner fails every Playwright gate.
  - **Why it's pre-existing:** the branch doesn't touch Playwright, and the slot-1 gate on the old `node_modules` passed the same suite that night.
  - **Current state:** slot 3's runner is in the drifted state now.
  - **Suggested:** bump the runner image's baked Playwright to the repo's pinned minor as an rl-infra image update. Until then, run `npx playwright install chromium` on a runner right after any `npm ci` there.

### 2026-09-27 — feat/rok-1366-magic-link-hardening (surfaced during fleet gate 8ed3cb1dfed3)
- **[med, time-of-day flake, pre-existing]** `scripts/smoke/calendar.smoke.spec.ts:282-290`: the ROK-1315 test "gameless event remains visible after the user picks \"None\"" failed on desktop and tablet with `expect(locator).toBeVisible() failed — .week-event-block … element not found`.
  - Cause: `sundayOf()` computes the week with `getUTC*`, but the calendar renders in the browser's local timezone (`CalendarView.tsx:52-56`, `calendar-view.utils.ts:49`), and the fleet runners set `TZ: America/Denver` (`rl-infra/docker-compose.yml:59,360`). Every week it fails Sun 04:00–06:00Z (05:00–07:00Z in winter) and Sat 22:00–24:00Z. Main CI runs in UTC and stays green.
  - Investigation: `planning-artifacts/INV-rok-1366-calendar.md`.
  - Suggested: compute the week in the browser (`page.evaluate` with local `getDay`) or navigate to `?view=week&date=<local date>`.

### 2026-09-28 — fix/notif-0928 (surfaced during TDB:899 prefs-resolution fix)
- **[med]** `api/src/notifications/discord-notification.service.ts:~331-352` (`autoDisableDiscord`): after repeated DM failures it writes `discord: false` only for the keys already present in the stored `channel_prefs` row. Every Discord send path now resolves prefs over `DEFAULT_CHANNEL_PREFS` (TDB:899), so a type missing from that row resolves to its default, and most defaults are discord ON. A user who was auto-disabled can therefore still get DMs for any type added after their row was written. This predates TDB:899: before it, a missing key was also read as "send".
  Suggested: build the disabled row from `resolveChannelPrefs(currentPrefs)` so that every `NOTIFICATION_TYPES` key gets `discord: false`, and add a unit assertion that the written row has a `discord:false` entry for every type.

### 2026-09-29 — fix/migration-tz-0929 (surfaced during TDB:1489)
- **[med]** Sibling zone-less `timestamp` columns left out of TDB:1489's scope (that fix converted only `community_lineup_schedule_slots.proposed_time` to `timestamptz`, migration 0194). Each one compared with `NOW()`/a `::timestamptz` param in SQL, or read back by a raw `db.execute`, shifts by the DB session TimeZone's offset on a non-UTC database: `api/src/drizzle/schema/community-lineups.ts:56` `target_date`, `:62` `voting_deadline`, `:63` `phase_deadline`, `:71` `auto_advance_paused_at`, `:78` `pending_advance_at`, `:166` `nomination_target_below_seen_at`, `:183` `nomination_target_disarmed_at`, `:198` `tie_detected_at`, `:208` `tie_expires_at`, `:210` `tie_expired_at`, `:218` `tie_pick_at`; `api/src/drizzle/schema/community-lineup-matches.ts:61` `threshold_notified_at`, `:103` `scheduling_submitted_at`. Invisible in prod only because the containers run UTC. Suggested: one migration per table, `SET DATA TYPE timestamp with time zone USING <col> AT TIME ZONE 'UTC'` (drizzle-kit omits the USING — hand-edit, as 0194 did), then grep raw readers for `to_char(`/`::text` and add `AT TIME ZONE 'UTC'`.
- **[low]** `events.duration` is a zone-less `tsrange`; raw readers still hand a bound back as a bare string that a consumer parses with `new Date()` as LOCAL time: `api/src/discord-bot/lfg-now/lfg-now.db-helpers.ts:39`/`:40` and `api/src/discord-bot/services/ephemeral-voice.db-helpers.ts:41`/`:42` (`endTime` parsed at `ephemeral-voice-idle.coordinator.ts:55`), `api/src/discord-bot/services/scheduled-event.db-helpers.ts:191`/`:192` (parsed at `scheduled-event.discord-ops.ts:268-269`/`:299-300` on the reconciliation path) and `:307` (parsed at `scheduled-event.gc.helpers.ts:68`/`:99`), `api/src/notifications/recruitment-reminder.helpers.ts:85` (parsed at `:134`, `:216`, `:238`, `:261`). `api/src/discord-bot/services/scheduled-event.capacity-recovery.integration.spec.ts:422` mirrors the gc transform on purpose. Already UTC-safe (a `Z` is appended before parsing, no action): `scheduled-event.revalidate.ts:67`/`:68` via `parseEventTimestampUtc` (`:52`), `ad-hoc-event.helpers.ts:61`, `api/src/lineups/standalone-poll/standalone-poll-notification.service.ts:159`. Bounds are also compared with `::timestamptz` params (e.g. `scheduled-event.db-helpers.ts:30`, `voice-attendance-flush.helpers.ts:91`, `recruitment-reminder.helpers.ts:95-96`), with `NOW()` (e.g. `api/src/lineups/ai-suggestions/voter-activity.helpers.ts:166`, `api/src/events/analytics-queries.helpers.ts:30`, `api/src/notifications/post-event-reminder.service.ts:99`) or with `NOW()::timestamp`, which is the session-local wall clock (`api/src/notifications/game-affinity-recipients.helpers.ts:59`, `recruitment-reminder.helpers.ts:121`). All of these depend on the host `TZ` / session TimeZone. TDB:1489 fixed only the two lineups `lockedInTime` readers (`scheduling-poll-state.helpers.ts`, `scheduling-poll-embed-data.helpers.ts`). Suggested: read bounds with `to_char(... '"Z"')` or a `Z`-appending parse (as `events.ts` `fromDriver` does), and compare against `::timestamp` params holding UTC wall-clock rather than `::timestamptz` / `NOW()`.
### 2026-09-29 — fix/itad-429-0930 (surfaced during review of the ITAD 429 fix)

- **low** `api/src/itad/itad-early-access-sync.helpers.ts:137` (`enrichEarlyAccessPhase`) / `api/src/itad/itad-price-sync.helpers.ts` (`processPricingChunks`) — no circuit breaker: in a sustained 429 storm every earlyAccess call and pricing chunk still waits out up to `ITAD_RETRY_AFTER_MAX_MS` per attempt, and the tail pass repeats the failures, so one run can take hours (overlap is now prevented by `waitForCompletion: true`). Suggested: stop the phase after K consecutive `ItadRetriesExhaustedError` / exhausted chunks and return `{ degraded: true }`.
- **nit** `api/src/itad/itad-early-access-sync.helpers.ts:69` (`withCallTimeout`) — a call given up on for a non-pause reason is not cancelled, so its retries continue in the background and can overlap the tail-pass request for the same game. Suggested: thread an `AbortSignal` through `getGameInfo` → `itadFetch` and abort it when the budget expires.
### 2026-09-29 — fix/discord-hygiene-0930 (surfaced while fixing the palette guard's comment stripper)

- **[low]** `api/src/discord-bot/embeds/personalized-surface.guard.spec.ts` and ~20 other source-scanning specs (api: `main.client-url.guard.spec.ts`, `common/timer-teardown.guard.spec.ts`, `discord-bot/discord-bot-permissions-literal.guard.spec.ts`, `lineups/tiebreaker/decided-game-writes.guard.spec.ts`, `common/contract-purity.spec.ts`, ...; web: `styles/*guard.test.ts`, `pages/lfg/*guard.test.ts`, `components/discord/read-only.guard.test.ts`, ...): each carries its own copy of the two-pass regex comment stripper (`/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g` then a `//` pass). It reads a comment marker inside a string or regex literal (`'a//b'`, `/^https?:\/\//`), or a slash-star inside a line comment, as a real comment and blanks the code after it, so a forbidden token placed in that span passes the guard. Nothing is hidden today, but the hole is open. `embed-colors.guard.spec.ts` now uses a literal-aware single pass whose output matched TypeScript's comment ranges on every `.ts` file under `api/src`.
  Suggested: move that `stripComments` into one shared test helper per workspace (for example `api/src/common/testing/strip-comments.ts` and a web twin) and point every guard at it.
### 2026-09-29 — fix/web-flows-0929 (surfaced during the fix/web-flows-0929 fleet gate)
- **[med]** `scripts/validate-ci.sh:133-136`: the runner install guard runs `npm ci` only when `node_modules/.bin/tsc` is not executable, so it catches a MISSING install but never a STALE one — a `package-lock.json` newer than `node_modules/.package-lock.json`. The slot-2 runner's `/workspace/node_modules` (`node_modules/.package-lock.json` dated 2026-09-05) predates #1377, `tsc` was present, the guard skipped `npm ci`, and Build failed with `vite.config.ts(53,17): error TS2307: Cannot find module 'rollup-plugin-visualizer'` (task 04885d5cff39). Pre-existing on any branch: the dependency landed on main in #1377 and the runner never reinstalled.
  Suggested: in the `--fleet` path, run `npm ci` when `package-lock.json` differs from `node_modules/.package-lock.json` (or always before Build).
- **[low]** `web/src/pages/onboarding-wizard-page.tsx:125-134` (`handleSkipAll`) and `:227` (`WizardHeader`): Skip All has no disabled / "Skipping…" state while its POST is in flight (repeat clicks are now silent no-ops), and a failed Complete or Skip All POST gives no feedback — neither `useCompleteOnboardingFte` (`web/src/hooks/use-onboarding-fte.ts:60`) nor either `mutate` call passes `onError`, and `web/src/lib/query-client.ts` has no global `MutationCache` error handler. Pre-existing UX gap, not introduced by the in-flight guard.
  Suggested: pass `isPending` to `WizardHeader` for a disabled Skip All with a "Skipping…" label, and add an `onError` toast to both calls.

### 2026-09-30 — fix/test-bot-a-0930 (surfaced during the test-bot smoke-fix review)
- **[med]** TDB:1076 is still open. `tools/test-bot/src/smoke/voice-fixtures.ts::pollClassifiedMetrics` now polls `/events/:id/metrics` before `rok943AssertMetrics` asserts, but that probably does not reach the recorded cause. `api/src/admin/demo-test-voice.helpers.ts:70-78` (`triggerClassifyForTest`) awaits `classifyEventSessions` and `autoPopulateAttendance` before POST `/admin/test/trigger-classify` returns 200, so the writes are committed before the first metrics read, and the poll will almost always exit on read #1. The flake (`attended: expected 4, got 0` after 2.8s) more likely comes from sessions that were never classified, or from grace handling.
  Suggested: when it recurs, read the `[ROK-943] await-processing settled` log line and the `[voice] … asserting on the last read: {…}` dump. Then check whether `rok943InjectSessions` wrote the sessions before classification ran.
- **[med]** TDB:1071 residual: `tools/test-bot/src/smoke/tests/private-lineup.test.ts` now archives only its own leftover lineups (`isLeftoverLineup`), but ten other smoke files still archive EVERY active lineup through their own `archiveAllLineups`, so they can still abort private-lineup's lineups (and each other's) mid-run at `SMOKE_CONCURRENCY > 1`. Affected: `lineup-abort.test.ts:76`, `lineup-grace-countdown.test.ts:54`, `lineup-deadline-voting-dm.test.ts:42`, `lineup-private-dm.test.ts:44`, `lineup-scheduling-poll-card.test.ts:56` (exported; its importers inherit it), `lineup-title.test.ts:31`, `lineup-tie.test.ts:38`, `lineup-channel-override.test.ts:35`, `lineup-tiebreaker-open.test.ts:51`, `public-share-link.test.ts:60`.
  Suggested: scope each file's archive to its own title prefixes via `isLeftoverLineup` (`tools/test-bot/src/smoke/lineup-milestone-match.ts:38`), as private-lineup does.
- **[nit]** `tools/test-bot/src/smoke/fixtures.ts:368` (`enableScheduledEvents`) swallows every error with `.catch(() => null)`. A failed enable therefore counts as a refcount hold in `scheduled-events-toggle.ts`, and the SE test times out waiting for a Scheduled Event that was never going to appear, instead of failing on the enable. The refcount's rejected-acquire rollback is unit-tested but only runs for an injected `enable`.
  Suggested: let `enableScheduledEvents` throw (the refcount already rolls back a rejected enable, and callers use `releaseScheduledEventsAndRethrow`), then run the SE smoke tests on a fleet env.

PASS in 45.4s (`Total: 1 passed, 0 failed, 1 total`, exit 0).
  Suggested: no code change; close the entry (n=1, passes).
- **[med]** TDB:966 — `tools/test-bot/src/smoke/tests/ai-chat.test.ts` (`SMOKE_NAME_FILTER='AI Chat'`, 12 tests, default `concurrency=5`): measured 3 times on env `test-bot-b-0930`, slot 1, at HEAD `bbf422c59`; **3/3 runs FAILED**, each on different tests, and a fourth diagnostic run at `SMOKE_CONCURRENCY=1` passed 12/12 (task `cf2a6ffac66b`).
  Run 1, task `e956714d4b39`: `Total: 11 passed, 1 failed, 12 total`; `FAIL AI Chat: Back returns to parent menu, Home returns to welcome (0.0s)` — `Events sub-menu missing Back button (ai:back). Got: []`.
  Run 2, task `8a5146569028`: `Total: 10 passed, 2 failed, 12 total`; `FAIL AI Chat: Session times out after 5 min inactivity -> welcome menu (0.1s)` — `Expected welcome menu (5+ buttons) after session expiry, got 0. This means the session was not properly expired.`; and the same Back/Home failure as run 1 (`Events sub-menu missing Back button (ai:back). Got: []`).
  Run 3, task `dc41db4a7c2d`: `Total: 10 passed, 2 failed, 12 total`; the same session-timeout failure as run 2; `FAIL AI Chat: Back returns to parent menu, Home returns to welcome (0.1s)` — `Home button did not return to welcome menu. Expected 5+ buttons, got 0. Components: []`.
  Probable cause (the n=1 concurrency=1 pass supports it, not proven): all twelve tests are `category: 'dm'` and drive one shared admin DM AI-chat session, so at the default `concurrency=5` the `sessionTimeout` test's `POST /admin/test/expire-ai-chat-session` and the Back/Home test's sub-menu navigation interleave and wipe each other's session state ("got 0 buttons" / `Components: []`). GitHub CI is not affected because `.github/workflows/discord-smoke.yml:169` sets `SMOKE_CONCURRENCY: '1'`; the targeted-run recipe (`reference_targeted_discord_smoke_recipe.md`) omits it, so a fleet filtered run uses the default 5 and reds spuriously.   Suggested: add `SMOKE_CONCURRENCY=1` to the targeted-run recipe (and to any brief that runs the smoke by name filter); separately, mark the AI Chat session tests serial in `tools/test-bot/src/smoke/run.ts`, or give each its own DM user, so the default-concurrency run stops lying. ### 2026-10-01 — fix/ci-tools-1001 (surfaced during B16)
- **[nit — correction of a false "resolved" claim]** `TECH-DEBT-BACKLOG.md:112` lists `~L907 — exec.ts:854 worktreePathSchema .refine TS2345/TS7006` as resolved ("`npx tsc --noEmit` in tools/mcp-rl-fleet now EXIT=0"). That was not true on `origin/main` (45b3c55e7): `tools/mcp-rl-fleet/src/exec.ts:852-858` still used the zod-3 `.refine(fn, (val) => ({ message }))` form while `tools/mcp-rl-fleet/package.json` now declares `zod: ^4.6.5` (root hoists 4.6.5). Under zod 4 that form is a TS2345/TS7006 type error AND a silent runtime regression — `normalizeParams` spreads the function to `{}`, so a bad `worktree_path` was rejected with a bare "Invalid input" instead of `validateWorktreePath`'s per-value reason (see the TDB:698 entry). Resolved by this branch: zod 4 `{ error: (iss) => ... iss.input ... }` form, pinned by `tools/mcp-rl-fleet/src/__tests__/worktree-path-schema.spec.ts`. Line 112 is left as written (append-only); this entry supersedes it.
  Suggested: none — fixed in fix/ci-tools-1001.

- **[low, follow-up — needs a design ruling]** `web/src/index.css:1231-1237`: the `[data-variant="quest-log"]` block sets `--color-dim: #a89070`, which reads about 2.75:1 on its own surface (`#faf3e0`), 2.42:1 on panel (`#f0e4c8`) and 2.16:1 on overlay (`#e8d8b0`). ROK-1472 AC1 asked for AA on surface, panel and overlay for the light AND quest-log schemes; the branch raised only the shared light dim, and `web/src/styles/faint-decorative.guard.test.ts` pins the default `light` scheme only. `sky`, `dawn`, `holy` and `celestial` also declare their own tinted `--color-dim` and were not measured against AA here.

- **[nit]** `web/src/styles/faint-decorative.guard.test.ts:81`: a failing `text-faint` use reports its line from `raw.indexOf(tag ?? m[0])`, the first textual occurrence in the file, so two identical tags both point at the first one. Counting newlines in the stripped code does not fix it as-is: `stripComments` in `web/src/test/form-primitives-count.ts:41-42` drops the newlines inside a block comment (block mode never emits `\n`), so lines drift after any multi-line JSDoc. Only the failure message is affected.
  Suggested: make the shared stripper emit `\n` in block mode (its consumers are the faint-decorative, raw-hue-light and form-primitives guards; re-run all three), then take the line from `code.slice(0, m.index)`.

### 2026-10-01 — fix/voice-join-investigation-1001 (ROK-1390 series quick-play smoke, fleet repro)
- **[med]** TDB:265 — `tools/test-bot/src/smoke/tests/series-dual-binding.test.ts:324` "ROK-1390: series-linked quick-play announces to series text channel, not #general". Bookkeeping: the "dup TDB:474" pointer on this item is off by two; the duplicate is TDB:476 (474 is the Scheduled-Event pollForCondition bullet).
  Repro: fresh env `vj1001-bindrefusal` (slot 2, image built from `2a2043044`), test-bot with the new /bind refusal check (`bindSeriesChannel` now throws `/bind <label> refused: <text>` when no BINDING SAVED embed comes back), exported `SMOKE_SKIP_VOICE_JOIN=0 SMOKE_CATEGORY=voice SMOKE_CONCURRENCY=1 SMOKE_NAME_FILTER=ROK-1390`, task `f8b1ae08081e`. Verbatim: `FAIL  ROK-1390: series-linked quick-play announces to series text channel, not #general (64.1s)` / `pollForEmbed timed out after 60000ms on channel 1475890045943812297`.
  Verdict: both /bind calls were accepted (no refusal thrown, the voice-binding row check passed), and the companion bot joined and left voice `GamerNight`. The earlier "Expected a series-linked voice binding ... got []" setup failure did not reproduce on a fresh env; the original pollForEmbed signature is back. The quick-play LIVE embed never reached the series text channel (#gamernight, which is also the env's default notification channel) within 60 s, so the fault sits after the bind is saved: the voice-state listener's binding lookup (see the cache hypothesis below), the game-voice-monitor spawn, or its series-announce routing.
  Whole voice category, same env and exports, same task: `Total: 11 passed, 1 failed, 12 total`; the only failure is the same ROK-1390 line (64.3s). That does not clear the earlier voice tests. They join the same voice channel, and the binding-cache hypothesis below is a cross-test effect.
  Loki (env stream, `container=rl-env-vj1001-bindrefusal-allinone`). Per-run timestamps were not kept and the env is destroyed, so each line's run is inferred where it can be. Neither run logged `Failed to create channel binding`. The two series `Bound channel` lines come from one of the two runs, and which one was not recorded: `Bound channel 1475890045943812297 in guild 1473686547122622724 as game-announcements (series: 3362b84d-f721-4be0-a6e3-2ebdde041af0)` and `Bound channel 1480010709155971193 in guild 1473686547122622724 as game-voice-monitor (series: 3362b84d-f721-4be0-a6e3-2ebdde041af0)`. After them no `[voice-gate] outcome=` line names the series binding, so the spawn guard never evaluated it. Two gate lines on the same voice channel name other game-voice-monitor bindings: `[voice-gate] outcome=below-threshold ch=1480010709155971193 binding=97885eb1-21e6-4881-90bb-9189cea0ccb1 purpose=game-voice-monitor game=32 min=99 counted=1 confirmed=0` and `[voice-gate] outcome=suppressed-scheduled ch=1480010709155971193 binding=a2639c0c-fb14-4ca7-99e9-f3fb4c12dc9a purpose=game-voice-monitor game=16`. These come from the whole-category run. `min=99` and the scheduled-event suppression match the two bindings that the "Multi-game channel detects second binding game event" test creates on that channel (`tools/test-bot/src/smoke/tests/voice-activity.test.ts:919-931`), and a fresh-env single-test run creates no such bindings. Whether they were logged at that test's own join or at the later ROK-1390 join was not recorded. If it was the ROK-1390 join, the gate evaluated a binding list cached before the series bind.
  First hypothesis, cheaper than a bisect: the voice-state listener's 60 s binding cache. `resolveAllBindings` caches each channel's bindings for `CACHE_TTL_MS` = 60 s (`api/src/discord-bot/listeners/voice-state.helpers.ts:7`, read at `:97-100`). The cache is cleared only by `clearAllState` (`api/src/discord-bot/listeners/voice-state.listener.ts:205`, on reconnect) and by the 10-minute sweep (`startBindingCacheSweep`, `api/src/discord-bot/listeners/voice-state-connect.helpers.ts:77`). Neither /bind nor the admin bindings API evicts it. So a channel resolved in the 60 s before the series bind keeps serving its old list for up to 60 s, which is the whole `pollForEmbed` window. In the whole-category run that old list would hold the multi-binding test's two bindings (deleted by then). In a single-test run it would be `[]` if a voice-state change on the shared GamerNight channel (another env's bot, or a person in CI's guild) resolved the channel first. The channel-presence service documents the same gap for its own cache and has a DEMO_MODE `forgetBinding` seam (ROK-1692, `api/src/discord-bot/services/channel-presence-embed.service.ts:298-309`). The voice-state listener has no such seam.
  Probe, as a one-off uncommitted change on the single-test recipe: (a) log a line on each `resolveAllBindings` cache hit (channel, entry age, binding ids) and rerun, or (b) hold the voice join until more than 60 s after the second bind. A cache-hit line without the series binding under (a), or a miss that goes away under (b), confirms it.
  Suggested: run the cache probe first. If it confirms, evict the voice-state binding cache on bind/unbind (or add a DEMO_MODE seam like `forgetBinding`) as its own change under `api/src/discord-bot/**`. Only if the probe is clean, bisect #1006-#1015 with the single-test recipe, one fresh env per probe, and read the env's Loki stream for the game-voice-monitor spawn/skip and `Bound channel` lines. Set `SMOKE_CHANNEL_SET` to the slot's channel set on those runs (this run fell back to the shared #gamernight / GamerNight channels in CI's guild).

### 2026-10-01 — fix/cron-write-amp-1001 (surfaced during review of the cron last_run_at deferral)
- **[low]** `api/src/main.ts` (no `app.enableShutdownHooks()`, and no SIGTERM handler anywhere in `api/src`): Nest never runs `onModuleDestroy` / `beforeApplicationShutdown` on a deploy or restart, so the API's destroy hooks do not run in production. One of them is `CronJobService.onModuleDestroy`'s final `flushLastRunUpdates()` (`api/src/cron-jobs/cron-job.service.ts:100-108`). Pre-existing: `git grep enableShutdownHooks origin/main -- api/src` finds nothing. On this branch the effect is bounded because only schedules of 15 minutes or less defer `last_run_at`, so a restart loses at most one queued value per job and the job's next run replaces it.
  Suggested: call `app.enableShutdownHooks()` in `main.ts` in its own PR, after auditing which destroy hooks would then start running on SIGTERM (BullMQ workers, the Discord client, deferred timers).

- **[nit]** `rl-infra/cli/rl:1083` (`install_runner_deps`) and `scripts/validate-ci.sh:708` (`_ensure_test_bot_deps`): `tools/test-bot` deps are still installed only when `tools/test-bot/node_modules` is missing. On a fleet runner that directory persists between claims, so a branch that changes `tools/test-bot/package-lock.json` still runs the Discord smoke and the render-rule self-test against stale deps. This is the TDB:924 bug class that `scripts/ci/ensure-runner-deps.sh` fixes for the root tree only.
  Suggested: let `ensure-runner-deps.sh` take a sub-root (e.g. `tools/test-bot`), with its own lock-protected lockfile-sha256 marker in that sub-root's `node_modules`, and call it from both places instead of the directory-exists guard.

TDB:547 stays open for those four.
  Suggested: rename the four remaining configs to `vitest.config.mts` in one mechanical commit, then grep `scripts/` and `.github/workflows/` for literal `vitest.config.ts` references to each.
### 2026-10-01 — fix/rok-1501-1001 (surfaced during review of the Cloudflare beacon CSP filter)
- **[nit]** `api/src/csp-report/csp-report-noise.helpers.ts:44-55` (`isCloudflareInsightsUri`): matches only on `new URL(uri).hostname`, so a report whose blocked-uri has no scheme (e.g. `static.cloudflareinsights.com`) or a trailing-dot FQDN (`cloudflareinsights.com.`) is not recognised and still reaches Sentry. Fails safe (beacon noise can leak; a real violation is never dropped), and current Chrome, Firefox and Safari send absolute URLs or origins for cross-origin script/connect violations, so no change is needed now.
  Suggested: only if Sentry later shows leftover beacon events with schemeless or trailing-dot blocked-uri values, add a host-only fallback (strip one trailing dot; parse a bare host as `https://<host>`) that keeps the dot-boundary check.
### 2026-10-01 — fix/flake-repro-1001 (flake reproductions, surfaced during the batch's repro-first protocol)
- **[med — still open; same race as TDB:1069 and TDB:1163]** `api/src/backup/backup.integration.spec.ts:475` (`restored dump has 0 rows in the 4 sanitized tables, > 0 in games`; the `COUNT(*)` loop is at `:471-476`, signature `Expected: 0 · Received: 4` on `app_settings`): not reproduced at n=50 on the current shard-2/4 composition at `2a2043044` (54 files, 496 tests, `--runInBand --randomize`, `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=4096`; fleet task ids `d1f48daf19a1`, `4a7f4f289aa4`, `d6ce7dfe9cb1`, `377416ebbe1a`, `edb3d9462992`). 50/50 runs exited 0, 54/54 suites and 496/496 tests passed every run, 50 distinct `--seed` values, and `backup.integration.spec` ran and passed (not skipped). 0 hits of "restored dump has 0 rows", 0 `socket hang up` / `ECONNRESET`. About 160-210s per run. This is weak evidence against the race: TDB:1163 records it on GitHub CI (PR #1144, run `34435759234`, shard 2/4 of 43 suites, failing on BOTH jest attempts in the job) with a different neighbour set (adding `game-activity-rollup.integration.spec.ts` shifted that shard), so 50 seeds over today's 54-file shard do not cover the trigger.

- **[low — still open]** TDB:512 — `api/src/lfg/lfg-offers.integration.spec.ts:114` (AC7b, test at `:93`; the positive control's Quick Play uses the Node clock `new Date()` at `:114`, while AC7c at `:131` already uses the M4 `+60s` offset; `spec-loop lfg-offers.integration 50 false`): not reproduced at n=50 on `2a2043044` (fleet, task id `6749f7ecc93d`). 50/50 runs exited 0, flake_count 0, 7/7 tests passed every run, and AC7b ran and passed (56 ms in run 1). 5-6s per run, with a few 20-46s outliers (runs 9 and 46) that still passed. An earlier attempt (task `7df7a7f9df0c`) was cancelled because it had no Redis, so it does not count toward n. Cumulative record: 1 failure in 71 runs (TDB:512's 1/21 local, then 0/50 here); no GitHub CI occurrence is recorded.

TDB:512's fix (derive the control's "after" instant from the intent's own `createdAt`, or mirror the M4 `+60s` offset from `:131` at `:114`) is cheap, but this is an integration test, so the repro-first rule holds it until a reproduction or an operator ruling.
- **[med — fixed by this branch]** TDB:325, with TDB:158 (ROK-1285/ROK-1290) and TDB:523 — `web/src/hooks/use-ai-suggestions.test.ts:195,295` (the two poll-cap tests; formerly real timers with 55_000 ms `waitFor` and 70_000 ms test budgets at old `:152-188` and `:251-285`): not reproduced at n=50 on `2a2043044` (fleet, task ids `ff62ea6f752f`, `03dfbe93550b`, `a763b49cc0ac`, `ebb472600d18`, `cfa3d537507e`; 50/50 exited 0, 6/6 tests every run, 98s per run). The run did measure the design problem: each poll-cap test took ~42s (42339 ms and 42143 ms in run 1) because `PENDING_POLL_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 15` × `PENDING_POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 3_000` (`web/src/hooks/use-ai-suggestions.ts:25-26`) is fixed wall clock, about 76% of the 55s budget on an idle runner. TDB:158 records the timeout under full-coverage load (`Test timed out in 70000ms`) and TDB:523 a deterministic `expected false to be true`. CLAUDE.md's repro-first STOP rule does not cover pure unit tests, so this branch moves both tests to fake timers (`vi.useFakeTimers({ shouldAdvanceTime: true })`, one `advanceTimersByTimeAsync(3_000)` per poll, waiting for each fetch result to render before the next tick) and replaces the 200 ms real sleep in the runaway check, which was shorter than one poll interval, with three full intervals. Per-test time drops from ~42s to ~0.4s. Mutation checks: `count > PENDING_POLL_MAX_ATTEMPTS` in the hook fails both tests with `expected false to be true`; lifting the `refetchInterval` cap fails the cap test with `expected 18 to be 15`.
  Suggested: none — fixed in fix/flake-repro-1001. If TDB:523's `expected false to be true` recurs on the new tests, suspect the hook's per-render poll count (`web/src/hooks/use-ai-suggestions.ts:104-118`) undercounting when two fetches commit in one render; that would be a product bug, not a test fix.

### 2026-10-01 — fix/rok-1696-1001 (surfaced during ROK-1696 suppression-window fan-out)

- **[med]** `@Optional() private readonly eventCache: ActiveEventCacheService | null` never resolves through production DI. Affected constructors: `api/src/discord-bot/services/event-auto-extend.service.ts:57`, `api/src/discord-bot/services/voice-attendance.service.ts:62`, `api/src/discord-bot/services/scheduled-event.service.ts:64`, `api/src/notifications/post-event-followup.service.ts:36`, `api/src/notifications/post-event-reminder.service.ts:38`, `api/src/notifications/event-reminder.service.ts:52`, `api/src/notifications/live-noshow.service.ts:45`. Under `emitDecoratorMetadata` with `strictNullChecks`, the union serializes to `Object` (a `ts.transpileModule` probe of `@Optional() a: A | null` emits `__metadata("design:paramtypes", [Object, ...])`). Nest therefore looks up the token `Object`, finds nothing, and `@Optional()` injects `undefined` without an error. Effect: every `this.eventCache?.invalidate()` / `refresh()` after an `extended_until` write is a no-op, and the cache short-circuits (for example `checkAndExtendEvents` returning early when nothing is active) never run, so those crons always query the DB. `api/src/discord-bot/services/scheduled-event.service.spec-helpers.ts:178-183` already notes the `Object` design type, but its claim that setting the field directly matches production DI is wrong. Why pre-existing: the constructors are unchanged on `b26acdc9d`. The new ROK-1696 fan-out helper keeps the cron path's existing `eventCache?.` calls and inherits the same no-op. Unit specs that construct the module through `Test.createTestingModule` with a `{ provide: ActiveEventCacheService }` entry also get `undefined`.
  Suggested: add an explicit token, `@Optional() @Inject(ActiveEventCacheService)`, in all seven constructors. Confirm each owning module can see the `EventsModule` export, then add one integration assertion that the injected cache is defined. Review the cron short-circuits before turning them on, because they have never run in production.

### 2026-10-01 — fix/rok-1696-1001 (voice binding cache never evicted on a binding write; surfaced by the ROK-1696 companion smoke)

- **[med — fixed by this branch]** `api/src/discord-bot/listeners/voice-state.helpers.ts:7,92-121` (`CACHE_TTL_MS = 60_000`, `resolveAllBindings`): the voice listener's per-channel binding cache was cleared only on bot connect/disconnect (`VoiceStateListener.clearAllState`). `ChannelBindingsService.bind` / `unbind` / `unbindById` / `updateConfig` emitted nothing, so for up to 60 s a newly created binding was ignored and a deleted one was still dispatched against. Observed: the ROK-1696 smoke (`tools/test-bot/src/smoke/tests/suppression-end-sync.test.ts`) binds voice channel 0 to gameA, joins, then deletes the binding; the ROK-959 smoke (`tools/test-bot/src/smoke/tests/voice-activity.test.ts:1227-1269`) re-binds the same channel to gameA and gameB inside the TTL, its join is dispatched only against the deleted gameA binding, and it fails with `extendedUntil not set on event <id>`. Hidden on main and in a full voice run because the preceding `multiGameVoiceDetected` test leaves a gameB binding in the cache. Pre-existing: the ROK-1696 fan-out changes neither the binding service, the cache nor the listener. ROK-1692 patched only the lobby presence service's copy of the cache (`ChannelPresenceEmbedService.forgetBinding`, a DEMO_MODE seam), so the listener's copy stayed open.
  Fixed by commit `9bf3f35b6`: the four service writes emit `channel-binding.changed` (`api/src/discord-bot/services/channel-binding-events.ts`) once per touched channel, including channels a series re-bind removed, and `VoiceStateListener.onBindingChanged` and `ChannelPresenceEmbedService.onBindingChanged` delete that channel's entry. Pinned by `voice-state.binding-cache.spec.ts`, `channel-bindings.service.spec.ts` and `channel-presence-embed.service.grace.spec.ts` (each revert-checked).
- **[low — still open]** `api/src/discord-bot/services/channel-bindings-invariant.helpers.ts:209` (game-merge binding normalization: `tx.delete(cb).where(inArray(cb.id, dropIds))` and the retarget updates after it) and FK cascades from a deleted game or series change `channel_bindings` outside `ChannelBindingsService`. They announce nothing, so both caches can still serve those rows for up to 60 s. Rare paths that self-heal at the TTL.
  Suggested: return the touched channel ids from the normalization and emit `CHANNEL_BINDING_EVENTS.CHANGED` after its transaction commits, not inside it.
- **[nit]** `ChannelPresenceEmbedService.forgetBinding` and its call at `api/src/discord-bot/demo-test-lobby-presence.controller.ts:78` are now redundant whenever the binding was written through `ChannelBindingsService`. Kept so the smoke seam never depends on event delivery.
  Suggested: drop the seam and its order pins (`demo-test-lobby-presence.controller.spec.ts:183-196`) only once the lobby presence smokes are green without it.

### 2026-10-02 — fix/voice-join-investigation-1001 (ROK-1390 series quick-play smoke re-run with #1429 in the image)
- **[med — still open, deterministic]** TDB:265 and its duplicate TDB:476 — `tools/test-bot/src/smoke/tests/series-dual-binding.test.ts:322` "ROK-1390: series-linked quick-play announces to series text channel, not #general". Re-run twice on a fleet env deployed from this branch merged with main at `d70995b1c`, which carries #1429 (`fa10b2f02`: the voice listener's binding cache is evicted when a binding changes). Result: FAIL, identically on both runs, so this is deterministic and not a flake. `npm run lint:no-sleep` passed (`OK: No sleep() calls found`, exit 0).
  Env: slot 2, image built from commit-sha `d70995b1c` (expected_head == synced_head == worktree HEAD). The branch is not pushed to origin; the deploy built from the synced worktree. The deploy took 27 min, 24 of it `npm ci`. Its `sync_settings` step FAILED with "laptop DB unavailable" while the VM settings-bundle overlay step PASSED; the bot identity was slot 2 (API bot `1544823771632762921`) and the test bot joined voice normally. Channel set slot-2: text `#slot-2-alt` and `#slot-2-fleet`, voice `#slot-2-voice`. `textChannels[0]` is `#slot-2-alt` (`1544868664929550356`), which is also the default notification channel, so the test's series text channel and the default channel are the same channel, and the quick-play embed landed in neither. The voice join was real: the test-bot output shows `[test-bot] Joined voice channel: slot-2-voice` and `Left voice channel`.
  Not recorded: fleet task ids, the verbatim failure line and any Loki lines for either run. This entry was written without fleet access and none of them were handed over, so none are reconstructed here. The last verbatim signature on file is the 2026-10-01 entry's `pollForEmbed timed out after 60000ms on channel <id>`; for this re-run only "no embed reached the series text channel or the default channel" is known.
  Verdict: the 60 s binding-cache hypothesis in the 2026-10-01 entry no longer explains the failure by itself. With #1429 the cache entry for the bound channel is deleted on every bind and config patch (`VoiceStateListener.onBindingChanged`, `api/src/discord-bot/listeners/voice-state.listener.ts:185-188`), and the result is unchanged. It survives only if `channel-binding.changed` is not delivered to that listener in the deployed image, which one log line in `onBindingChanged` settles.
  Next hypotheses, from reading `d70995b1c`; none has been probed on a run yet. They are ordered by what one more run can tell apart:
  1. The test cannot see what happened in the channel. With series text channel == default channel on this env, a spawned embed that fails the poll predicate (`e.author` matching `/quick play/i`, `series-dual-binding.test.ts:355`, written for ROK-1447/ROK-1459 before the Quick Play builder and the channel-level presence embed were reworked, `api/src/discord-bot/services/ad-hoc-notification.service.ts:54,313`) looks identical to no embed at all. Probe: on timeout, put the last ~10 messages of the series text channel (author, title, footer) in the failure text, the same way `bindSeriesChannel` now reports a `/bind` refusal, and rerun once.
  2. Sibling suppression. `ensureNotSuppressed` (`api/src/discord-bot/services/ad-hoc-event.service.ts:162`) suppresses on any live, uncancelled, non-ad-hoc event for the bound game that has no ephemeral channel and no series voice binding (`buildAnchoredGameClause`, `api/src/discord-bot/services/ad-hoc-suppression.helpers.ts:121`) or whose binding is a sibling on the channel (`siblingSubquery`, `:190`). The test's own series event starts in 60 min (`tools/test-bot/src/smoke/fixtures.ts:59`) so it cannot be the suppressor, but a seeded event for `firstGame`, or one an earlier voice test left with `extended_until` in the future, can. Loki signature: `[voice-gate] outcome=suppressed-scheduled ... binding=<series voice binding>` plus `[voice-spawn] suppressed binding=... event=<id> match=...` (`ad-hoc-suppression.helpers.ts:271`). The original 2026-08 observation recorded no gate line at all, so this alone does not fit that observation.
  3. The gate never evaluates the series binding. `resolveAllBindings` (`api/src/discord-bot/listeners/voice-state.helpers.ts:92`) filters `getBindingsWithGameNames(clientService.getGuildId())` by channel id; if the guild id on the `Bound channel ... in guild <id>` line differs from the API bot's primary guild on slot 2, the series binding is invisible to the voice listener while every other check passes.
  Suggested: one more run on slot 2 with (a) the channel-dump failure text from hypothesis 1, (b) a temporary log in `onBindingChanged` and in `resolveAllBindings` (channel, resolved binding ids), and (c) the Loki filter `container=rl-env-<slug>-allinone |~ "voice-gate|voice-spawn|Bound channel|channel-binding"` with per-run timestamps kept this time, `SMOKE_CHANNEL_SET=slot-2` exported. Branch on the result: an embed present with a different author means fix the poll predicate (1); `suppressed-scheduled` means fix the fixture's game or clean the suppressing event (2); no gate line means hypothesis 3 or an undelivered event. Do not bisect #1006-#1015 before that run. Keep TDB:265 and TDB:476 open until a clean fleet voice run passes the test.

### 2026-10-02 — fix/rok-1161-api-phase8-wf3-1002 (surfaced while fixing review findings on the api spec noUncheckedIndexedAccess ratchet)

- **[med]** Two TypeScript versions typecheck the api workspace, and they disagree. `api/package.json:100` pins `typescript: ^6.0.3`, installed nested as `api/node_modules/typescript` 6.0.3 (`package-lock.json:141`). The root hoists 5.9.3 (`package-lock.json:20003`). CI (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:180`, plus the full-project step above it) and `scripts/validate-ci.sh:650` run `npx tsc` from the repo root, so they get 5.9.3. Run from `api/`, `npx tsc` gets 6.0.3. There `types` defaults to `[]`, so no jest globals load (`src/version/version.controller.spec.ts(186,3): error TS2593: Cannot find name 'it'`, about 23.8k cascades), and `strict` defaults on. With `--types jest,node` added, 6.0.3 still reports errors that 5.9.3 does not:
  `api/src/sentry/environment-snapshot.service.spec.ts(206,25)` and `(229,25): error TS2352: Conversion of type 'EventProcessor' to type '(event: Record<string, unknown>) => Record<string, unknown>' may be a mistake` (both `tsconfig.json` and `tsconfig.spec-nuia.json`), and
  `api/src/discord-bot/services/ad-hoc-reaper.service.spec.ts(102,52): error TS2345: Argument of type '(name: string | symbol) => true' is not assignable to parameter of type '(event: string | symbol | event[], ...values: any[]) => boolean'` (`tsconfig.json` only; `discord-bot/**` is still excluded from the spec ratchet).
  Pre-existing: both files are byte-identical to `origin/main`, and the repo-root (5.9.3) runs of both configs exit 0. The same skew hid `api/src/backup/backup.service.spec.ts:77,289` TS2345 (a `(name: string) => string` callback over `string | undefined`) from CI. That one only fires once `strictFunctionTypes` is on; it is fixed on this branch.
  Suggested: pick one compiler for the api workspace. Either drop the api pin to the root version, or typecheck with the workspace's own tsc and set `types` and `strict` (or `strictFunctionTypes`) explicitly in `api/tsconfig.json`. Fix the three errors above in the same change.

### 2026-10-02 — fix/b70-testcontainers-12-1002 (@testcontainers/postgresql 12.2.0 bump; review nits)

- **[nit]** `package.json:4-5`, `api/package.json:4-5` (`"engines": {"node": ">=22.0.0"}`): with `@testcontainers/postgresql` at `^12.2.0`, the effective Node floor for the API integration suite is **22.22**, not 22.0. `testcontainers@12.2.0` declares `"node": ">= 22.22"`, and the hoisted `undici@8.11.2` it pulls in declares `">=22.19.0"`. `.npmrc` does not set `engine-strict`, so on Node 22.0-22.21 npm prints `EBADENGINE` and installs anyway. The integration suite can then fail at import time, the same class of failure as the `webidl.util.markAsUncloneable is not a function` error recorded for PR #1142 in the 2026-09-11 entries. CI (`node-version: 22`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml:142` and the other setup-node steps) and both Dockerfiles (`node:22-alpine`) float to the latest 22.x, so neither is exposed. Laptops and the fleet runner are exposed, and the runner's Node minor is unverified (see the 2026-10-02 runner Playwright base nit above). Engines were left at `>=22.0.0` on purpose, so that a devDependency bump does not change what a production install accepts.
  Suggested: assert `node --version` >= 22.22 as a hard gate before any fleet testcontainers run. Raise both engines fields to `>=22.22.0` in a separate change once the runner is confirmed at >= 22.22.
- **[nit]** `CLAUDE.md:256` (Local CI, "Escalate to `--full`") says GitHub's path filter treats dependency files as `code` (lint) but not `api`/`web`, so GitHub skips unit and integration for a deps-only change. That has been stale since #1073 (2026-09-02): `.github/workflows/ci.yml:83-92` lists `api/package.json` and `package-lock.json` under `api`, and `web/package.json` and `package-lock.json` under `web`, so unit and integration do run. Escalating a dependency change to `--full` is still right, for a different reason. GitHub integration sets `DATABASE_URL` to a Postgres service (`ci.yml:532`), which short-circuits the global setup (`api/src/common/testing/integration-global-setup.ts:37`). On GitHub, testcontainers is therefore reached only by the two backup integration specs that start their own container (`backup.helpers.loud-failure` and `backup.helpers.restore-via-runboot`). The same stale claim in the `.github/dependabot.yml` @nestjs ignore comment was corrected on this branch. CLAUDE.md is operator config and was not edited.
  Suggested: reword the sentence to give the Postgres-service reason above.
- **[nit]** `package-lock.json` (`node_modules/jsdom/node_modules/undici` removed): the bump dedupes jsdom, which is web's vitest environment, onto the hoisted `undici@8.11.2` instead of its nested 8.10.2, which is within jsdom's `^8.10.2` range. A checkout that ran `npm install` instead of `npm ci` keeps the nested 8.10.2 on disk (`npm ls undici` shows `jsdom@30.1.1` -> `undici@8.10.2`), so its local web checks do not match the lockfile. `npm ci` on the fleet and on GitHub does match it.
  Suggested: no change. If the web vitest shards misbehave after this lands, check jsdom's undici moving from 8.10.2 to 8.11.2 first.

### 2026-10-02 — fix/shell-test-pipefail-1002 (surfaced by the #1443 CI lint job, run 37072482975)

- **[low — flaky, fixed here]** `scripts/test/validate-ci-fleet-flag.test.sh:83` (`assert_out_matches`; the same pipe sits at `:90` `assert_out_absent` and `:97` `assert_err_matches`, line numbers at `f0e8e198e`), with identical helpers at `scripts/test/validate-ci-only-flags.test.sh:78,85,92` and `scripts/test/ensure-runner-deps.test.sh:50`. The CI lint job's "Shell behavior tests (scripts/test)" step printed `printf: write error: Broken pipe` and three `FAIL [validate-ci-fleet-flag.test.sh::AC2: the summary lists every step] <row label>: output did not match '<pattern>'` lines. The three row labels were not handed to this branch; that is the helper's own `fail` text. Mechanism: the helpers ran `printf '%s' "$INVOKE_OUT" | grep -E -q -e "$pattern"` under `set -uo pipefail`. `grep -q` exits on its first match, so when the captured output is large (as on CI) printf is still writing, gets EPIPE, and pipefail makes the pipeline non-zero. A MATCH then reads as a miss, and `assert_out_absent` would wrongly pass. Locally the output is ~15 KB and 89/89 passed. Resolved by this branch: each of those helpers now runs `grep -E -q -e "$pattern" <<<"$VAR"` with the pattern unchanged, and each file gains a harness self-check that runs its output helpers against a 1 MiB output whose match is on line 1. Revert-checked: with the old pipe every self-check flips, for example `assert_out_matches on a 1 MiB output: expected pass, got fail`. Same-class pipes left unchanged because their inputs are small: `echo "$X" | grep -q` under pipefail at `scripts/test/reconcile-migrations-trust-probe.test.sh:247,281,321,367`, `scripts/test/sync-local-to-env.test.sh:148`, `scripts/test/rl-reencrypt-settings.test.sh:59,67,83,92` and `scripts/test/validate-ci-skip-contract.test.sh:75,77,85,93,101,118`.
  Suggested: switch those to here-strings the next time each file is touched.

The channel dump has no Quick Play embed at all. Loki shows `[voice-bind] resolve <voice> cache=miss bindings=[<id>:game-voice-monitor:series=<rg>]` at the bot's join, with no `[voice-gate]` or `[voice-spawn]` line after it in either window. Verdict: none of H1-H4. H3 and H4 are disproven (the binding is resolved and the eviction events arrive). H2 is disproven (no `suppressed-scheduled`). H1 is disproven (the embed was never posted). Mechanism: the companion bot is the only voice member. Its join reaches `spawnAllConfirmed` (`api/src/discord-bot/listeners/voice-state-gate.handlers.ts:170,184`), because `getGameFilteredCount` counts bots by design (`voice-state.handlers.ts:339-359`, MED-2) and the /playing override confirms the game. `handleGameSpecificGroupRoster` then skips every bot member (`voice-state-recovery.handlers.ts:130`, ROK-1445 AC9, #1069 `56c141a7b` 2026-09-01) and returns false, and `spawnAllConfirmed:214` returns without a trace. So the test has been unreachable with the companion bot since #1069, the same class already recorded at `voice-activity.test.ts:170-175,486-490` and `demo-test-lfg-now-voice.controller.ts:5-10`. CI never ran it (`discord-smoke.yml:166` SMOKE_SKIP_VOICE_JOIN=1). Residual: the Loki regex omitted `Voice state error`. Suggested: (1) revert the [voice-bind] diagnostics `e4510db86` (done on this branch); (2) trace the bot-only branch as `[voice-gate] outcome=no-human-members` (done, pinned in `voice-state.bot-filter.spec.ts`); (3) drive the smoke through the DEMO_MODE seam `POST /admin/test/quick-play/voice-join` (added on this branch) with a seeded Discord-linked user and drop its `canJoinVoice` gate so CI runs it.

### 2026-10-02 — fix/b42-discord-off-prefs-1002 (review follow-ups left open)

- **[med]** TDB:1956 residual gap — `api/src/notifications/notification-mapping.helpers.ts:62` (`buildDiscordDisabledPrefs`) with `:41` (`resolveChannelPrefs`) and `api/src/notifications/discord-notification.service.ts:352` (`disableDiscordForAllTypes`): auto-disable now stores Discord OFF for every type in `NOTIFICATION_TYPES` at the moment it runs. A notification type added in a LATER release is missing from that stored row, so `resolveChannelPrefs` fills it from `DEFAULT_CHANNEL_PREFS` (Discord on for most types), and an already auto-disabled, unreachable user starts getting DMs for it again. Each failure then counts toward a fresh auto-disable, which only re-closes the gap until the next new type. The prefs JSONB has no way to say "Discord off for every type, including future ones."

The test bot has no library, wishlist or heart rows for the event's game, so the invite renders no personalized fields, and `loadPugInviteData` trims to two upstream (`toFields`, `api/src/discord-bot/services/pug-invite-personalization.helpers.ts:106`) before `buildPugInviteEmbed`'s own `MAX_PERSONALIZED_FIELDS` cap (`api/src/discord-bot/services/pug-invite.helpers.ts:171`) runs. ### 2026-10-02 — fix/b44-lineups-tidy-1002 (surfaced during TDB:920 atomic tiebreaker start)

- **[low]** `api/src/lineups/tiebreaker/tiebreaker.service.ts:109-111` (`TiebreakerService.start`): the "one tiebreaker at a time" check (`findAndValidateLineup` + `assertNoActiveTiebreaker`) reads `communityLineups.activeTiebreakerId` OUTSIDE the transaction that now creates the row, with no row lock, and `community_lineup_tiebreakers` has no unique or partial-unique index on `lineup_id` (the tiebreakers table itself has no unique constraint; the file's three uniques in `api/src/drizzle/schema/community-lineup-tiebreakers.ts`, `uq_tiebreaker_matchup_round_pos`, `uq_tiebreaker_bracket_vote` and `uq_tiebreaker_veto_user`, are on matchups, bracket votes and veto votes). Two concurrent `POST /lineups/:id/tiebreaker` calls (creator and operator, or a double submit) can therefore both pass the check and both commit an `active` row; the lineup links whichever committed last, and the other stays `active` but unlinked. `findPendingOrActiveTiebreaker` (`api/src/lineups/tiebreaker/tiebreaker-query.helpers.ts:33-44`) filters on `status != 'dismissed'` (so it returns `resolved` rows too, not only pending/active ones) and has `limit(1)` with no `orderBy`, so later reads and votes may land on the orphan. Reasoned from the code, not reproduced. TDB:920 made each start atomic but deliberately did not add a lock for this race.
  Suggested: re-read the lineup `FOR UPDATE` on the start transaction's `tx` and run `assertNoActiveTiebreaker` there, or add a partial unique index on `(lineup_id) WHERE status IN ('pending','active')`.

### 2026-10-02 — fix/b50-validate-ci-reporting-1002 (resolutions)

- **[resolved 2026-10-02]** TDB:1455 (`scripts/validate-ci.sh`, `E2E_SCOPE=none` + stop-on-first-failure before Discord smoke). `--only-e2e` + `E2E_SCOPE=none` already skipped Playwright on main; the real residual was `--fleet`'s implied `e2e_mode=on` overriding `none`, plus the abort after a Playwright FAIL that hid the Discord smoke row. Now only an explicit `--with-e2e` (`e2e_explicit`) overrides `none`, and a Playwright FAIL is recorded and deferred (`RUN_STEP_DEFER_FAIL`, set around that one step) so Discord smoke still runs; the gate then prints the summary and exits 1, never "All checks passed!". Every other step stays fail-fast. Covered by four `TDB:1455` cases in `scripts/test/validate-ci-fleet-flag.test.sh`.
- **[resolved 2026-10-02]** TDB:330 (`scripts/validate-ci.sh` integration step reported an OOM as a plain `FAIL` with no hint of how much of the suite never ran). `run_integration_tests` now tees every shard's jest output to a `mktemp` file outside the worktree; when any shard fails it prints `N spec(s) never ran or did not finish:` and the first 40 paths (`... and M more`), diffing `find src -name '*.integration.spec.ts'` against the ANSI-stripped `PASS`/`FAIL` result lines, or a `setup failure?` hint when no spec reported at all. An all-PASS run prints nothing extra. Shard count, fast-fail break, heap pin and the Redis sidecar are unchanged. Covered by two `TDB:330` cases in `scripts/test/validate-ci-integration-shards.test.sh`.
- **[resolved 2026-10-02]** TDB:2088 (`tools/test-bot` deps installed only when its `node_modules` was missing, so a fleet runner kept a stale tree after a test-bot lockfile change). `scripts/ci/ensure-runner-deps.sh` takes an optional `SUBDIR` (relative, no `..`; capability token `RL_DEPS_SUBROOT_V1`): on a runner it re-installs `ROOT/SUBDIR` whenever `SUBDIR/package-lock.json`'s sha differs from `SUBDIR/node_modules/.rl-lockfile-sha256`, under the same `/tmp/rl-npm-ci.lock`; on a laptop it still installs only when `node_modules` is missing. `validate-ci.sh` routes the test-bot install through it (the `npm install` fallback stays outside the helper, with no marker), and `rl-infra/cli/rl` `install_runner_deps` calls it when the checkout's helper carries the token, else keeps the old missing-dir guard. Covered by cases (s1)-(s6) in `scripts/test/ensure-runner-deps.test.sh`.

### 2026-10-02 — fix/b47-itad-breaker-abort-1002 (review nit left open)

- **[nit]** `api/src/itad/itad-http.util.ts:148-157` (`requestWithRetry`) with `api/src/itad/itad-rate-limit.util.ts:62-74` (`acquireItadSlot`): a call aborted while it waits in the `acquireItadSlot` FIFO still takes its pacing turn (`waitForTurn`), and only the post-acquire `signal.aborted` check then returns `EXHAUSTED`. The backoff sleep after a 5xx is not interrupted by the signal either. No request goes out, so this is not a correctness bug, but every abandoned early-access call spends one shared pacing slot that a live call could have used. Follow-up to TDB:1985. Suggested: pass the `AbortSignal` into `acquireItadSlot` and set `ticket.abandoned` on abort (the flag already skips `waitForTurn`), and race the backoff sleep against the signal.

- **[low]** The branch moved the scheduled-event, ephemeral-voice, LFG-now, recruitment-reminder and game-affinity readers onto shared UTC fragments (`utcIsoText`, `utcWallClock`, `NOW_UTC` in `api/src/drizzle/timestamp-utils.ts`). These TDB:1981 sites still compare the zone-less `events.duration` / `extended_until` with a `timestamptz`, so each window shifts by the DB session's UTC offset on a non-UTC session:
  - `api/src/discord-bot/services/voice-attendance-flush.helpers.ts:91`/`:92`, `:111`/`:112`, `:151` (`${now.toISOString()}::timestamptz`)
  - `api/src/lineups/ai-suggestions/voter-activity.helpers.ts:166` (`now() - interval ...`)
  - `api/src/events/analytics-queries.helpers.ts:30`, `:110`, `:127`, `:179` (`<= NOW()`)
  - `api/src/notifications/post-event-reminder.service.ts:99` (`BETWEEN (now() - ...) AND (now() - ...)`)
  
  Suggested: swap each for `utcWallClock(...)` / `NOW_UTC` from `timestamp-utils`, and add a `SET LOCAL TIME ZONE 'America/New_York'` integration case per reader, as `scheduled-event.db-helpers.tz.integration.spec.ts` does.
- **[nit]** Inline copies of the ISO-Z `to_char` reader remain in `api/src/lineups/scheduling/scheduling-poll-state.helpers.ts:91`, `scheduling-poll-embed-data.helpers.ts:86`, `scheduling-unanimous.helpers.ts:78` and `scheduling-rally.helpers.ts:213`. ### 2026-10-02 — fix/b45-lineups-timestamptz-1002 (surfaced during TDB:1980)

- **[low]** `api/src/drizzle/schema/community-lineup-tiebreakers.ts:46`: `round_deadline` is still a zone-less `timestamp`. Found while converting the sibling lineup and match columns to `timestamptz` in migration 0197; deliberately left out of that change. Two places in `api/src/lineups/lineup-tiebreaker-reminder.helpers.ts` read it:
  - `:94` compares it with `${now.toISOString()}::timestamp`. That comparison does not depend on the session TimeZone today: Postgres drops the zone suffix when it casts a literal to `timestamp`, and the only writer is a JS Date (`api/src/lineups/tiebreaker/tiebreaker-lifecycle.helpers.ts:68`, serialised as a UTC wall clock), so both sides are UTC wall clocks. It stays correct only while every writer is a JS Date; a SQL `NOW()` writer would stamp the session zone's wall clock and drift by that zone's offset.
  - `:104-107` (`normalizeTiebreakerRow`) runs `new Date(r.roundDeadline)` on the raw naive string that `db.execute` returns. V8 parses a zone-less `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS` in the API PROCESS's local zone, so on any non-UTC host the deadline shifts by the host's offset. This is the live hazard.
  Suggested: convert the column like 0197 (`USING "round_deadline" AT TIME ZONE 'UTC'`), switch the `:94` cast to `::timestamptz`, and parse at `:107` with `parseTimestampUtc` (`api/src/drizzle/timestamp-utils.ts`), all in one PR.
- **[nit]** `api/src/discord-bot/services/scheduled-event.revalidate.ts:48-53` (`parseEventTimestampUtc`): now that `parseTimestampUtc` (`api/src/drizzle/timestamp-utils.ts:27`) accepts the same short offsets (`/[+-]\d{2}(:?\d{2})?$/`), the two helpers are near-duplicates. The only differences are that `parseEventTimestampUtc` trims its input and maps `null`/empty to an Invalid Date.
  Suggested: make `parseEventTimestampUtc` a thin wrapper (trim + null/empty guard) over `parseTimestampUtc`, and keep its existing unit cases.
